## CS_GLORIA_1  (anh dinh: khong)
Photorealistic character reference sheet, 16:9, of one person: front view, three-quarter view, profile, and four expressions (neutral, proud smile, hurt, tearful laugh). The grandmother: a 71-year-old Black American woman with medium-deep brown skin and warm red-gold undertones. A round face with full cheeks, warm brown eyes, a broad soft nose and full lips; her skin has few deep wrinkles for her age: soft smile lines, gentle creases at the outer corners of her eyes, slightly deeper-toned skin under her eyes and a few tiny raised deep-brown spots on her upper cheeks, as many older Black women have. Short natural silver-white hair in tight coils, cut in a neat tapered shape, close at the sides and back and fuller on top. Reading glasses with thin gold frames hang on a fine chain against her chest; small gold stud earrings, a small gold cross necklace and a thin gold wedding band. Medium height, a soft, solid build and an upright posture: the proud, healthy, quick-witted matriarch of her family, never frail. She wears a copper-colored silk blouse, a pearl necklace, a cream cardigan and a floral cotton apron. Brown skin true to life: each person keeps their own skin depth and undertone, rich and natural, with soft highlights on the forehead, cheekbones and nose and visible detail in the shadows; palms lighter than the backs of the hands; never grey, ashy, washed out, orange or lightened; every face has enough soft light to read its expression.
